# Scope completion ledger — 2026-11-24

Audit timestamp: **2026-11-24T16:18:02.427997+00:00** (UTC; client date November 24).

**136 complete requirement records; 66 remain open.** All 202
original IDs are retained. Completed records leave the open queue, not history.
The 39 packages and 117 subtasks remain separately tracked: broad package
completion cannot be inferred from one functioning feature. Separately tracked
enhancements are attached to existing packages, without expanding or replacing
the adopted tag taxonomy.

## How to read the percentages

0% = no milestone evidenced; 20% = bounded written contract; 40% = implemented
mechanism; 60% = executable positive/negative checks; 80% = complete selected
integration; **100% = all required acceptance for the full named item functioning
and evidenced**. These are equally weighted milestone percentages, not effort,
accuracy or a project-wide percentage. Draft scope alone earns at most 20%.
Independent accuracy, human accessibility and actual phones are credited only
when performed. Tags have their own gates. Shared capabilities and policy records
are not automatically released tags.

Completion timestamps are when this audit verifies completion, not invented
historical finish times. Prior implementation attribution comes from session/run
records; Claude's initial additions are attributed from the owner's report.
Codex performed this audit and repaired the research components. A Git account
name does not identify which agent wrote the code. Current source checks and
historical public/runtime observations remain distinguishable in evidence.

No overall average is calculated: records overlap, costs differ, grouping records
are not additional products, and 20% planning is not 20% of business delivery.
Future source/route/acceptance changes reopen affected records.

| TAG-NF — Reference-supported claims | 20% | No implementation credited; Codex planned | Define claim units, reference freshness and corpus identity.; Implement claim-to-passage mapping with exact support evidence and explicit missing-corpus behavior.; Test paraphrases, conflicting sources, stale references and adversarial retrieval; expose source dependencies. Final acceptance: Independent claim/passage labels measure support accuracy and unsupported-claim coverage.; No corpus means unsupported/unavailable for NF, not an NF verdict.; Changing a source invalidates only findings derived from that source. Owner: Codex | [NF-01.md](../docs/tags/NF-01.md) |
| TAG-FI — Reference contradiction and citation problems | 20% | No implementation credited; Codex planned | Separate contradiction and unresolved-citation evidence types.; Implement passage comparison and reproducible citation-resolution scope.; Test fictional examples, quotations, incomplete corpora and legitimate but absent references. Final acceptance: Blind labels distinguish contradiction from missing evidence.; Offline non-resolution alone never establishes that a source does not exist anywhere.; Publish error rates by evidence type; NF and FI may describe different claims in one answer. Owner: Codex | [FI-01.md](../docs/tags/FI-01.md) |
| TAG-HP — Hallucination indicators | 20% | No implementation credited; Codex planned | Evaluate specificity, contradiction, repetition and hedging independently.; Define when indicators can be combined; track shared causes instead of counting them as independent votes.; Test satire, creative work, repetition, uncertain language and consistent unsupported statements. Final acceptance: Independent labels and comparison with simpler baselines establish useful indicator performance.; The release claim names the supported indicator; it does not infer model intent.; No source lookup capability is misrepresented as a factual verification. Owner: Codex | [HP-01.md](../docs/tags/HP-01.md) |
| TAG-MT — Manipulative framing | 20% | No implementation credited; Codex planned | Write an annotation guide separating persuasion, urgent safety advice and coercive framing.; Implement indicator/context evidence and a dismissible explanation.; Obtain blind multiple-human labels and adjudicate disagreements. Final acceptance: Report agreement, category errors and confidence calibration separately.; Existing alpha 0.55 and accuracy values remain candidates until the protocol is accepted.; MT disagreement never blocks PS, provenance or privacy tag release. Owner: Codex | [MT-01.md](../docs/tags/MT-01.md) |
| TAG-PS — Command and instruction risk | 20% | No implementation credited; Codex planned | Finish the independent sampling/labeling protocol for context-v5 without changing its current meaning.; Run the frozen holdout and outside-user workflow checks; retain the preview if gates fail.; Separately specify credential-exfiltration and typosquat enhancements; do not advertise them from existing command tests. Final acceptance: Use PS-01 and its 17-scenario acceptance packet plus phone/device checks.; Candidate 95% Wilson lower-bound precision 0.93 and recall 0.75 require prior protocol acceptance and independent labels.; Maintain all 86 current regression cases and publish category failures; no scam-intent inference. Owner: Codex | [PS-01.md](../docs/tags/PS-01.md), [PS-01.md](../docs/tags/PS-01.md), [PS-01-acceptance.md](../docs/tags/PS-01-acceptance.md), [2026-10-08-upgrade-device-public.json](../runs/2026-10-08-upgrade-device-public.json), [2026-10-08-ps-routing-gates.json](../runs/2026-10-08-ps-routing-gates.json) |
| TAG-IV — Independent human review | 20% | No implementation credited; Codex planned | Specify reviewer consent, review scope and attestation identity.; Implement subject/version binding and review/signature verification.; Test modified subjects, impersonation, withdrawn review, expired evidence and conflicts. Final acceptance: A machine never assigns IV merely because a detector passed.; Changing the subject or review scope invalidates that attestation.; Review identity and signature integrity are tested separately from the reviewed claim’s accuracy. Owner: Codex | [IV-01.md](../docs/tags/IV-01.md) |
| TAG-FA — Full AI contribution provenance | 20% | Claude — initial bridge draft; Codex — repaired typed/subject/conflict research seam; no media adapter or released tag | Define acceptable generation events and manifest claims with signer trust.; Verify provenance binding, continuity and ambiguous/missing-history handling.; Test copied human text, spoofed manifests, missing events and transformed subjects. Final acceptance: Absent typing, paste bursts or missing human evidence never establish FA.; Valid signatures establish the signer/record, not the truth of its origin claim.; Independent provenance cases include assisted-input users and incomplete capture.
